   969	def reset_for_tests() -> None:
   970	    """Re-create REGISTRY so tests start from clean counters."""
   971	    if not _AVAILABLE:
   972	        return
   973	    global REGISTRY, decision_total, decision_resolve_seconds
   974	    global pipeline_step_seconds, provider_failure_total, provider_latency_seconds
   975	    global model_mapping_violation_total
   976	    global sse_subscribers, sse_dropped_total, workflow_step_total
   977	    global auth_login_total, subprocess_orphan_total, persist_failure_total
   978	    global sandbox_image_rejected_total, sandbox_lifetime_killed_total
   979	    global sandbox_launch_total, sandbox_output_truncated_total
   980	    global sandbox_oom_total
   430	    # Phase 47D: DecisionEngine timeout sweep (30 s cadence)
   431	    from backend import decision_engine as _de
   432	    sweep_task = asyncio.create_task(_de.run_sweep_loop())
   433	    # Phase 52: Webhook DLQ retry worker
   434	    from backend import notifications as _notif
   435	    dlq_task = asyncio.create_task(_notif.run_dlq_loop())
   436	    # R9 row 2940 (#315): P3 → L1 log + email digest periodic flusher.
   437	    # Singleton-guarded; when SMTP is unconfigured the loop still runs
   438	    # (it falls back to a structured log line per flush) so the sweep
   439	    # is always cleanly drained on shutdown.
   440	    digest_task = asyncio.create_task(_notif.run_email_digest_loop())
   441	    # Phase 63-D: Daily IQ benchmark loop (opt-in L3, gated by env).
   442	    from backend import iq_nightly as _iq
   443	    iq_task = asyncio.create_task(_iq.run_nightly_loop())
   444	    # Phase 65 S4: Fine-tune nightly loop (opt-in L4, gated by env).
   445	    from backend import finetune_nightly as _ft
   446	    ft_task = asyncio.create_task(_ft.run_nightly_loop())
   447	    # Phase 63-E: Memory decay loop (opt-in L3, gated by env).
   448	    from backend import memory_decay as _md
   449	    md_task = asyncio.create_task(_md.run_decay_loop())
   450	    # Z.2 (#291): LLM provider balance refresh loop — 10 min cadence,
   451	    # exponential backoff to 1 h on failure. Writes each supported
   452	    # provider's BalanceInfo to SharedKV("provider_balance") so the
   453	    # upcoming GET /runtime/providers/{provider}/balance endpoint can
   454	    # serve cached values without per-request vendor calls.
   455	    from backend import llm_balance_refresher as _lbr
   456	    balance_task = asyncio.create_task(_lbr.run_refresh_loop())
   457	    # MP.W16.2: subscription account expiry monitor. Reuses provider
   458	    # adapter health checks and emits best-effort alerts when a CLI
   459	    # subscription is inactive, expired, or inside the warning window.
   460	    from backend.agents import subscription_account_monitor as _sam
   461	    subscription_monitor_task = asyncio.create_task(_sam.run_monitor_loop())
   462	    # KS.2.5: per-worker CMEK revoke detector. Each worker polls the
   463	    # same external KMS/Vault source on a <=60s cadence; the in-process
   464	    # latest snapshot is observability-only until KS.2.6/KS.2.11 wire
   465	    # graceful 403s and durable revoke events.
   466	    from backend.security import cmek_revoke_detector as _cmek_revoke
   467	    cmek_revoke_task = asyncio.create_task(_cmek_revoke.run_detection_loop())
   468	    # I6: DRF per-tenant sandbox capacity grace deadline sweep
   469	    from backend import sandbox_capacity as _sc
   470	    drf_task = asyncio.create_task(_sc.run_sweep_loop())
   471	    # M2: per-tenant disk quota sweep (5 min) — emits SSE soft warnings
   472	    # and triggers LRU cleanup when over soft threshold.
   473	    from backend import tenant_quota as _tq
   474	    quota_task = asyncio.create_task(_tq.run_quota_sweep_loop())
   475	    # Q.6 #300 checkbox 3: dedicated user_drafts 24h retention sweep.
   476	    # Opportunistic GC on PUT only bounds active typers; idle workers
   477	    # need this explicit loop to prevent stale drafts from lingering.
   478	    from backend import user_drafts_gc as _ud_gc
   479	    drafts_gc_task = asyncio.create_task(_ud_gc.run_gc_loop())
   480	    # Y6 #282 row 6: hourly workspace GC reaper. Walks the row-1
   481	    # five-layer hierarchy, moves stale leaves (mtime older than
   482	    # ``keep_recent_workspaces_stale_days``, agent already finished,
   483	    # no fresh ``.git/index.lock``) to ``_trash/`` for cool-down,
   484	    # hard-deletes trash entries past ``workspace_gc_trash_ttl_days``,
   485	    # and pre-emptively LRU-evicts older workspaces when a tenant is
   486	    # over hard quota. Singleton-guarded per worker; cross-worker
   487	    # races are idempotent (rename / rmtree). See
   488	    # ``backend/workspace_gc.py`` module docstring for the audit.
   489	    from backend import workspace_gc as _ws_gc
   490	    workspace_gc_task = asyncio.create_task(_ws_gc.run_gc_loop())
   491	    # M4: cgroup per-container sampler → per-tenant Prometheus gauges +
   492	    # billing accumulator. Lifespan-scoped so it starts with the app
   493	    # and stops cleanly at shutdown.
   494	    from backend import host_metrics as _hm
   495	    host_metrics_task = asyncio.create_task(_hm.run_sampling_loop())
   496	    # H1: whole-host ring buffer (60 × 5s snapshots = 5 min history).
   497	    # Feeds the AIMD capacity planner + the GET /host/metrics endpoint.
   498	    host_ringbuf_task = asyncio.create_task(_hm.run_host_sampling_loop())
   499	    # BP.R.6: per-worker RTK install health gauge. The Dockerfile
   500	    # hard-fails missing RTK, but this keeps the running image visible
   501	    # to Prometheus after deploy.
   502	    from backend import rtk_observability as _rtk_obs
   503	    _rtk_obs.probe_install_status()
   504	    # G7 (HA-07): flip the backend_instance_up gauge to 1 once the
   505	    # app is fully booted. Shutdown flips it back to 0 so the
   506	    # reverse-proxy drops this replica from rotation before the
   507	    # in-flight drain window.
   508	    from backend import ha_observability as _hao
   509	    _hao.mark_instance_up()
   510	    yield
   511	    try:
   512	        _hao.mark_instance_down()
   513	    except Exception as exc:
   514	        _log.debug("[lifecycle] mark_instance_down failed: %s", exc)
   515	    # G1: graceful drain — flip gate, flush SSE, wait in-flight (30 s),
   516	    # close DB. Background tasks are cancelled AFTER the drain so any
   517	    # in-flight request that depends on them still has a chance to
   518	    # finish within the timeout.
   519	    try:
   520	        result = await _lifecycle.graceful_shutdown(close_db=False)
   521	        _log.info("[lifecycle] graceful_shutdown result: %s", result)
   522	    except Exception as exc:
   523	        _log.warning("[lifecycle] graceful_shutdown raised: %s", exc)
   524	    for t in (
   525	        pubsub_task, watchdog_task, sweep_task, dlq_task, digest_task,
   526	        iq_task, ft_task, md_task, balance_task, subscription_monitor_task,
   527	        cmek_revoke_task, drf_task, quota_task, drafts_gc_task,
   528	        workspace_gc_task, host_metrics_task, host_ringbuf_task,
   529	    ):
   530	        t.cancel()
   531	        try:
   532	            await t
   533	        except asyncio.CancelledError:
   534	            pass
   535	    await _ss.close()
   536	    # Close the asyncpg pool before the SQLite dev handle.
   537	    # Order matters: any background task still writing via the pool must
   538	    # finish first (_lifecycle.graceful_shutdown above has already drained
   539	    # in-flight requests and background tasks were cancelled), then the
   540	    # pool releases its underlying TCP connections, then ``db.close()``
   541	    # tears down the aiosqlite WAL (no-op on the PG path since
   542	    # ``db.init()`` is a no-op there post-Step-C.2).
   543	    try:
   544	        from backend import db_pool as _db_pool
   545	        await _db_pool.close_pool()
   546	    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover — defence in depth
   547	        _log.warning("[lifecycle] db_pool.close_pool raised: %s", exc)
   548	    try:
   549	        app.state.error_aggregation.close()
   550	    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover — defence in depth
   551	        _log.debug("[lifecycle] error aggregation close failed: %s", exc)
   552	    await db.close()

   565	# CORS — configurable origins (hardcoded localhost only in debug mode)
   566	_cors_origins = [settings.frontend_origin]
   567	if settings.extra_cors_origins:
   568	    _cors_origins.extend(o.strip() for o in settings.extra_cors_origins.split(",") if o.strip())
   569	if settings.debug:
   570	    # Dev convenience: add localhost variants
   571	    for dev_origin in ["http://localhost:3000", "http://127.0.0.1:3000", "http://localhost:3001"]:
   572	        if dev_origin not in _cors_origins:
   573	            _cors_origins.append(dev_origin)
   574	app.add_middleware(
   575	    CORSMiddleware,
   576	    allow_origins=_cors_origins,
   577	    allow_credentials=True,
   578	    # M2 audit (2026-04-19): explicit method + header allowlist. With
   579	    # `allow_credentials=True` a `*` value silently fails spec compliance
   580	    # on some browsers AND lets an attacker pair stolen origins with
   581	    # forged X-Forwarded-For / X-Tenant-Id if the reverse proxy ever
   582	    # gets misconfigured. Listing only what legitimate UI + fetch calls
   583	    # need closes that gap without breaking the app. Extend if a new
   584	    # header ever becomes necessary — do NOT re-introduce `*`.
   585	    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
   586	    allow_headers=[
   587	        "Accept",
   588	        "Accept-Language",
   589	        "Authorization",
   590	        "Content-Type",
   591	        "Idempotency-Key",
   592	        "X-CSRF-Token",
   593	        "X-Requested-With",
   594	    ],
   595	    expose_headers=["X-CSRF-Token"],
   596	)
   597	
   598	# S2-9 (#354): secure-by-default auth baseline. After this middleware
   599	# loads, every non-allowlisted path requires a valid session.
   600	# Defaults to MODE=log (advisory — just logs would-be-blocks). Flip
   601	# to enforce via `OMNISIGHT_AUTH_BASELINE_MODE=enforce` once the
   602	# allowlist has been validated against production traffic. The
   603	# allowlist lives in backend/auth_baseline.py next to the middleware
   604	# so one code review covers the whole policy.
   605	#
   606	# Registration order matters: added BEFORE ha_observability so the
   607	# 5xx-rate counter doesn't see spurious "no-session 401s" from
   608	# allowlist tuning. This middleware sits between CORSMiddleware
   609	# (above) and ha_observability + routers (below).
   610	from backend import auth_baseline as _auth_baseline
   611	_auth_baseline.install(app)
   612

   614	# rate metric. Registered here so it wraps ALL other middleware +
   615	# routers — every response, including error responses produced by
   616	# Starlette's default handlers, gets recorded.
   617	from backend import ha_observability as _ha_observability
   618	_ha_observability.register_middleware(app)

     1	# backend/requirements-dev.in — dev-time tooling dependencies.
     2	#
     3	# FX.6.7 (2026-05-04): peeled out of ``backend/requirements.in`` so the
     4	# production lockfile (``requirements.txt``, consumed by Dockerfile.backend
     5	# + every prod-shaped CI job) no longer carries test runners and lint /
     6	# audit CLIs that the runtime never imports.
     7	#
     8	# The split:
     9	#   * ``backend/requirements.in``  — runtime imports only (FastAPI,
    10	#     SQLAlchemy, Alembic, asyncpg, anthropic, httpx, …). httpx STAYS
    11	#     in prod because backend code (47 files: backend/security/oauth_*,
    12	#     backend/email_delivery/*, backend/storage_provisioning/*, …)
    13	#     imports it directly at runtime — only ``pytest`` / ``respx`` are
    14	#     truly test-only.
    15	#   * ``backend/requirements-dev.in`` — pytest stack (test runners) +
    16	#     respx (httpx mock; no prod import) + pip-audit / ruff / mypy
    17	#     (FX.6.5 lint/audit toolchain, no runtime import path).
    18	#
    19	# Resolution / install pattern (pip-tools constraint mode):
    20	#   * pip-compile reads ``-c requirements.txt`` so any transitive dep
    21	#     shared with the prod lockfile (e.g. ``httpx`` pulled in by
    22	#     respx, ``typing-extensions`` pulled in by mypy) is pinned to the
    23	#     EXACT prod version + hashes. No ``==`` / ``--hash`` divergence
    24	#     between the two lockfiles.
    25	#   * Dev / CI test environments install BOTH:
    26	#       pip install --require-hashes -r backend/requirements.txt
    27	#       pip install --require-hashes -r backend/requirements-dev.txt
    28	#   * Production (Dockerfile.backend, openapi-contract job, backend-
    29	#     migrate job, frontend-e2e job) installs ONLY ``requirements.txt``
    30	#     and never sees pytest / respx / ruff / mypy / pip-audit.
    31	#
    32	# Regeneration:
    33	#   pip-compile --quiet --allow-unsafe --generate-hashes \
    34	#       --output-file=backend/requirements-dev.txt \
    35	#       backend/requirements-dev.in
    36	#
    37	# (CI's lockfile-drift gate runs the same command and `git diff
    38	# --exit-code` on the result; out-of-band edits to requirements-dev.txt
    39	# fail the build.)

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Release-train readiness + operations handoff (2026-05-22)
     2	
     3	**Audience**: operators + agents picking up the single-trunk Release Train. This is the SINGLE entry point — the model, the verified mechanism, the runbooks (cut a release / operate staging / signing), the key facts, and the remaining activation items. Companion to ADR-0040 (the decision), ADR-0042 (registry/distribution), and `[[reference_runner_operations_sop]]` (the runner fleet).
     4	
     5	> **TL;DR**: every release-train MECHANISM was hand-exercised end-to-end on 2026-05-22 and works — candidate build → staging deploy (systemd-daemon, survives restart) → /readyz + deploy-overlay → staging gate → **promote-by-digest** → **cosign signing**. What stands between here and a hands-off auto-release is a short list of **activation items** (below), not unknowns.
     6	
     7	---
     8	
     9	## 1. The model (ADR-0040)
    10	- **`develop` is the only long-lived branch.** No `main`. Runner `feature/*` → `refs/for/develop` → Gerrit +2 → submit.
    11	- **A release = a promoted IMAGE TAG + digest** (RT-20 image-tag-only): NO `v*` git tag. Candidate images `sha-<fullsha>` are built from a chosen green develop SHA; promotion **retags the validated digest** to `vX.Y.Z` in the GitLab CR (no rebuild → identical digest).
    12	- **Sole registry = private GitLab CR `sora.services:49160/omnisight/omnisight-productizer`** (ADR-0042). GHCR decommissioned (prod + host run zero GHCR images). On-prem distribution would need a public registry later (ADR-0042 trigger).
    13	
    14	## 2. Verified mechanism (what was hand-exercised 2026-05-22)
    15	| Step | Mechanism | Status |
    16	|---|---|---|
    17	| Candidate build | `sha-<fullsha>` backend+frontend → CR (pipeline-API `CANDIDATE_SHA`, or `build_image.sh`) | ✅ built+pushed manually |
    18	| Bundle digest-seal | bundle.json carries the real post-build digests (OP-1607) | ✅ flows through deploy+promote |
    19	| Staging deploy | dedicated `omnisight-staging` stack, systemd-managed | ✅ healthy; **survives full restart (47s) + restart-storm** |
    20	| /readyz + deploy-overlay | RT-08 overlay lock → `/api/version` deployed_tag/digests; gate fail-closed | ✅ overlay populates real values; **gate 503s fail-closed on a bad lock** |
    21	| Staging gate | smoke/canary green evidence over the candidate digests | ✅ `assert_staging_gate_passed` matches digests |
    22	| **Promote-by-digest** (RT-12) | `imagetools create` retag validated digest → `vX.Y.Z`, verify same digest | ✅ **`vX.Y.Z` resolved to the exact candidate digest, no rebuild** |
    23	| **cosign signing** | key-based attestation, verifies against `deploy/cosign/cosign.pub` | ✅ signs + verifies (OP-1610) |
    24	
    25	---
    26	
    27	## 3. RUNBOOK — cut a release
    28	Prereqs: a green `develop` SHA; `COSIGN_KEY`/`COSIGN_PASSWORD` exported (see §6); GitLab CR login (`docker login sora.services:49160`).
    29	1. **Pick the SHA**: a develop commit with green CI (until RT-04a auto-records green, choose by hand).
    30	2. **Build the candidate** (both images), tag `sha-<fullsha>`, push to CR:
    31	   `docker build -f Dockerfile.backend -t sora.services:49160/omnisight/omnisight-productizer/backend:sha-<sha> .` (+ frontend) → `docker push`. (In CI this is the pipeline-API `candidate-*` jobs, which also cosign-sign + seal the bundle.)
    32	3. **Seal the bundle** with the real digests (CI does this; manual = a bundle.json with `images.{backend,frontend}.digest`).
    33	4. **Deploy to staging** (§4) on `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=sha-<sha>` → verify `/readyz`=200 + `/api/version` overlay shows the real digest.
    34	5. **Run the staging gate** (smoke) → green JSONL evidence over the candidate digests (`scripts/staging_gate.py`).
    35	6. **Promote**: `python3 scripts/promote_image_bundle.py --bundle <sealed> --from staging --to vX.Y.Z --actor <you> --approval-refs <JIRA> --registry sora.services:49160/omnisight/omnisight-productizer --staging-evidence <green.json>` → retags the digest to `vX.Y.Z` + cosign-attests + writes the release_train row. (Verify: `vX.Y.Z` resolves to the candidate digest.)
    36	7. **Deploy prod** by the promoted tag/digest (the existing prod deploy SOP `[[reference_prod_deploy_sop]]`).
    37	
    38	## 4. RUNBOOK — operate staging
    39	- **Authority compose**: `deploy/staging/docker-compose.yml` (PG-backed, GitLab CR). Env: `deploy/staging/.env` (gitignored, staging-only secrets; `OMNISIGHT_REGISTRY`=49160, `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG`=the candidate, `OMNISIGHT_ADMIN_PASSWORD`/`DECISION_BEARER`, `OMNISIGHT_LLM_PROVIDER=ollama`).
    40	- **Daemon (boot-survivable)**: `omnisight-staging-compose.service` (user systemd, **enabled**, `WantedBy=default.target`, Linger=yes). `systemctl --user {start,restart,stop,status} omnisight-staging-compose.service`. Project = `omnisight-staging`, ports 8010/8011/55432/18080, isolated PG/volumes — never collides with prod.
    41	- **Fresh DB init (one-time)**: the backend will NOT bootstrap an empty DB (integrity probe `exit 78`). On a brand-new PG volume, run once before first boot:
    42	  `docker run --rm --network omnisight-staging_default -e OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://omnisight:<pw>@postgres:5432/omnisight_staging --entrypoint sh <backend-image> -c "cd /app/backend && python -m alembic -c alembic.ini upgrade head"`. (The PG volume persists across reboot, so this is once-per-fresh-volume only.)
    43	- **Deploy-overlay lock** (RT-08): `scripts/write_deploy_overlay_lock.py --bundle <sealed> --tag <tag> ... --out $OMNISIGHT_DEPLOY_OVERLAY_DIR/deploy-overlay.lock` (writer now chmod 644, OP-1609). Compose mounts `$OMNISIGHT_DEPLOY_OVERLAY_DIR:/etc/omnisight:ro`. Set `OMNISIGHT_REQUIRE_DEPLOY_OVERLAY=1` to make `/readyz` enforce it. A lock change needs the backend recreated (`up -d --force-recreate backend-a backend-b`, OP-1609) to be re-read.
    44	- **Verify**: `curl http://localhost:18080/readyz` (caddy ingress) → 200; `curl .../api/version | jq` → real `deployed_tag`/`deployed_digest_*`.
    45	- **Health audit**: `bash scripts/deployment-audit.sh` (1 expected fatal = `sora-bridge-sync`, tracked OP-1608).
    46	
    47	## 5. RUNBOOK — teardown / rebuild staging
    48	- Tear down (keep volumes): `docker compose -p omnisight-staging -f deploy/staging/docker-compose.yml down`. Add `-v` to wipe the DB (then re-do the fresh-DB init).
    49	- The PG image MUST be `omnisight-postgres-pgvector:16-alpine` (schema needs the `vector` extension; vanilla `postgres:16-alpine` fails — OP-1605).
    50	- Backends need a writable `/app/data` volume (`staging-data`) for the secret key (read_only rootfs — OP-1605).
    51	
    52	## 6. RUNBOOK — cosign signing (key-based, ADR-0040 #5 reconciled)
    53	- **The signing key is self-managed (NOT keyless)**: private `~/.config/omnisight/cosign/cosign.key`, password `~/.config/omnisight/cosign/cosign-password.txt`, public = committed `deploy/cosign/cosign.pub`.
    54	- Sign/promote env: `export COSIGN_KEY=~/.config/omnisight/cosign/cosign.key COSIGN_PASSWORD="$(cat ~/.config/omnisight/cosign/cosign-password.txt)"`.
    55	- The promote attests with `--key` + `--tlog-upload=false` (private; no public Sigstore Rekor leak) + a URI predicate-type (OP-1610).
    56	- Verify an attestation: `cosign verify-attestation --key deploy/cosign/cosign.pub --type https://omnisight.dev/attestation/image-promotion/v1 --insecure-ignore-tlog <image@digest>`.
    57	- ⚠ `build_image.sh` default `OMNISIGHT_COSIGN_KEY` path is stale (`cosign-private-key`) — set `OMNISIGHT_COSIGN_KEY=~/.config/omnisight/cosign/cosign.key` for build-time signing.
    58	
    59	## 7. Key facts / locations
    60	- Registry: `sora.services:49160/omnisight/omnisight-productizer/{backend,frontend,installer}` (claude-bot = Maintainer, can push).
    61	- Prod: compose `docker-compose.prod.yml` (project `omnisight-productizer`), PG-HA `omnisight-pg-primary`/`-standby` (pgvector), runs `v0.5.0-rc5-hotfix4`.
    62	- Control plane: `/home/user/sora-bridge/` (separate checkout) runs the gerrit-jira-bridge heartbeat + pipeline-coordinator + merger-bot — **never delete it**; it's stranded on `main`@rc1 (OP-1608).
    63	
    64	## 8. Remaining activation items (the gap to hands-off auto-release)
    65	| Item | Ticket | What |
    66	|---|---|---|
    67	| Green-evidence producer | RT-04a (in OP-1603 family) | CI records a green row per develop SHA so auto-cut picks a SHA without a human |
    68	| Candidate-build CI auto-trigger | (scope:release-train) | pipeline-API auto-builds a candidate on a green SHA |
    69	| cosign creds in CI / build-sign | OP-1610 follow-ups | `OMNISIGHT_COSIGN_KEY` path fix; CI `--tlog-upload=false`; promote inline-verify needs build-signed candidates |
    70	| Control-plane re-point off main | **OP-1608** | sora-bridge → develop/release-tag (the 1 honest audit fatal) |
    71	| RT-08b/13b/16/17 | filed | overlay/rollback/dry-run/cutover evidence — now reachable on the live staging |
    72	
    73	## 9. Gotchas this sprint learned (don't re-discover)
    74	- **Archived ≠ resolved**: runner blocker-check only treats `公開済み`/Published as done; close [OP] tickets at 公開済み, never Archived ([[reference_runner_operations_sop]]).
    75	- **pgvector PG image** + **writable /app/data** + **fresh-DB `alembic upgrade head`** are required for staging to boot (§4/§5).
    76	- **Overlay lock must be 644** (container uid 65532 reads it) + a lock change needs `--force-recreate` (OP-1609).
    77	- **Ungraceful backend crash → ~5% ingress blip** until caddy ejects (graceful rolling-restart avoids it; possible caddy passive-health tuning).
    78	- **cosign ≥2.x rejects bare predicate-type strings** — use a URI (OP-1610).
    79	
    80	## 10. See also
    81	- ADR-0040 (single-trunk release train), ADR-0041 (merger conflict triage), ADR-0042 (image distribution).
    82	- `docs/operations/runner-operations-sop.md`, `docs/operations/2026-05-22-staging-dry-run-tabletop-and-gap-inventory.md`, `docs/operations/release-train-hotfix-policy.md`, `docs/operations/release-train-actor-protection.md`.
    83	- Memory: `[[project_audit19_staging_buildout]]`, `[[project_retire_main_blast_radius_audit]]`, `[[reference_prod_deploy_sop]]`.

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
     3	# OmniSight — Production 零停機部署腳本
     4	#
     5	# 在 Ubuntu-24.04 (Production WSL) 上執行
     6	# 實現 G2 rolling restart：一次只重啟一個 backend replica
     7	#
     8	# 使用方式（RT-07a — 單一主幹 release train）：
     9	# 一次部署只接受「最終發行身分」：final tag (vX.Y.Z) 或 image digest。
    10	# 不再有 branch 部署、不再 default 到 main。
    11	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --tag=v1.2.0            # 部署 final release tag
    12	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --digest=sha256:<64hex> # 依 image digest 部署
    13	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --tag=v1.2.0 --skip-build
    14	#                                                    # 跳過 build（image 已存在）
    15	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --tag=v1.2.0 --dry-run  # 只印步驟不執行
    16	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --tag=v1.2.0 --gerrit-source=gerrit
    17	#                                                    # 從 Gerrit remote fetch（預設自動偵測）
    18	#   ./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --tag=v1.2.0 --alembic-mode=pg-clone
    19	#                                                    # 在 PG clone 上 dry-validate migrations
    20	#
    21	# 沒有 --insecure-skip-verify escape hatch（RT-07a 移除）：授權新 ref 的
    22	# 唯一稽核途徑是 PR 修改 allowlist + 簽署 final tag，或部署 cosign 驗證過
    23	# 的 image digest。
    24	# ━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
    25	
    26	set -euo pipefail
    27	
    28	COMPOSE_FILE="docker-compose.prod.yml"
    29	TAG=""
    30	DIGEST=""
    31	SKIP_BUILD=false
    32	DRY_RUN=false
    33	GERRIT_SOURCE="${OMNISIGHT_GERRIT_SOURCE:-}"
    34	ALEMBIC_MODE="${OMNISIGHT_ALEMBIC_MODE:-apply}"
    35	HEALTH_RETRIES=30
    36	HEALTH_INTERVAL=3
    37	
    38	RED='\033[0;31m'; GREEN='\033[0;32m'; YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
    39	CYAN='\033[0;36m'; BOLD='\033[1m'; NC='\033[0m'
    40	log()  { echo -e "${GREEN}✅${NC} $*"; }
    41	warn() { echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠️${NC}  $*"; }
    42	err()  { echo -e "${RED}❌${NC} $*"; exit 1; }
    43	step() { echo -e "\n${CYAN}${BOLD}━━━ $* ━━━${NC}\n"; }
    44	
    45	# ── CLI Args ──
    46	for arg in "$@"; do
    47	    case "$arg" in
    48	        --tag=*) TAG="${arg#*=}" ;;
    49	        --digest=*) DIGEST="${arg#*=}" ;;
    50	        --skip-build) SKIP_BUILD=true ;;
    51	        --dry-run) DRY_RUN=true ;;
    52	        --gerrit-source=*) GERRIT_SOURCE="${arg#*=}" ;;
    53	        --alembic-mode=*) ALEMBIC_MODE="${arg#*=}" ;;
    54	        --alembic-pg-clone) ALEMBIC_MODE="pg-clone" ;;
    55	        --help|-h)
    56	            echo "Usage: $0 (--tag=vX.Y.Z | --digest=sha256:<64hex>) [--skip-build] [--dry-run] [--gerrit-source=REMOTE] [--alembic-mode=apply|pg-clone]"
    57	            exit 0 ;;
    58	        *) err "Unknown argument: $arg" ;;
    59	    esac
    60	done
    61	
    62	# RT-07a: a production deploy identity is a FINAL tag (vX.Y.Z) or an
    63	# image digest — never a branch, and never an implicit default. Require
    64	# exactly one. The shape of each value is enforced by check_deploy_ref.sh.
    65	if [ -n "$TAG" ] && [ -n "$DIGEST" ]; then
    66	    err "--tag and --digest are mutually exclusive — a deploy has exactly one final identity"
    67	fi
    68	if [ -z "$TAG" ] && [ -z "$DIGEST" ]; then
    69	    err "release-train: a final deploy identity is required — pass --tag=vX.Y.Z or --digest=sha256:<64hex>. Branch deploys (and the implicit main default) were removed in RT-07a."
    70	fi
    71	
    72	case "$ALEMBIC_MODE" in
    73	    apply|pg-clone) ;;
    74	    *) err "Unknown --alembic-mode=$ALEMBIC_MODE (expected apply or pg-clone)" ;;
    75	esac

    77	_run() {
    78	    if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
    79	        echo "  [dry-run] $*"
    80	    else
    81	        eval "$@"
    82	    fi
    83	}
    84	
    85	_run_cmd() {
    86	    if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
    87	        printf '  [dry-run]'
    88	        printf ' %q' "$@"
    89	        printf '\n'
    90	    else
    91	        "$@"
    92	    fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	_detect_gerrit_source() {
    96	    if [ -n "$GERRIT_SOURCE" ]; then
    97	        git remote get-url "$GERRIT_SOURCE" >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
    98	            err "--gerrit-source=$GERRIT_SOURCE is not a configured git remote"
    99	        printf '%s\n' "$GERRIT_SOURCE"
   100	        return 0
   101	    fi
   102	
   103	    if git remote get-url gerrit >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   104	        printf 'gerrit\n'
   105	        return 0
   106	    fi
   107	
   108	    local remote
   109	    while IFS= read -r remote; do
   110	        local url
   111	        url="$(git remote get-url "$remote" 2>/dev/null || true)"
   112	        case "$url" in
   113	            *gerrit*|*sora.services*|*29418*)
   114	                printf '%s\n' "$remote"
   115	                return 0 ;;
   116	        esac
   117	    done < <(git remote)
   118	
   119	    err "No Gerrit git remote found. Add a 'gerrit' remote or pass --gerrit-source=<remote>; refusing to fetch from a possibly stale mirror."
   120	}
   121	
   122	_upsert_env() {
   123	    local key="$1"
   124	    local value="$2"
   125	    if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
   126	        echo "  [dry-run] set $key=$value in .env"
   127	        return 0
   128	    fi
   129	    if [ ! -f .env ]; then
   130	        warn ".env missing; skip frontend freshness metadata persistence"
   131	        return 0
   132	    fi
   133	    if grep -qE "^#?${key}=" .env; then
   134	        local escaped
   135	        escaped=$(printf '%s' "$value" | sed 's/[\/&]/\\&/g')
   136	        sed -i "s/^#\\?${key}=.*/${key}=${escaped}/" .env
   137	    else
   138	        printf '\n%s=%s\n' "$key" "$value" >> .env
   139	    fi
   140	}
   141	
   142	_env_file_value() {
   143	    local key="$1"
   144	    grep -E "^${key}=" .env 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true
   145	}
   146	
   147	_prod_database_url() {
   148	    local url="${SQLALCHEMY_URL:-${OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL:-${DATABASE_URL:-}}}"
   149	    if [ -z "$url" ]; then
   150	        url="$(_env_file_value SQLALCHEMY_URL)"
   151	    fi
   152	    if [ -z "$url" ]; then
   153	        url="$(_env_file_value OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL)"
   154	    fi
   155	    if [ -z "$url" ]; then
   156	        url="$(_env_file_value DATABASE_URL)"
   157	    fi
   158	    case "$url" in
   159	        postgresql://*|postgresql+*://*) printf '%s\n' "$url" ;;
   160	        "") err "--alembic-mode=pg-clone requires SQLALCHEMY_URL, OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL, or DATABASE_URL in env/.env" ;;
   161	        *) err "--alembic-mode=pg-clone requires a PostgreSQL URL, got scheme from configured DB URL" ;;
   162	    esac
   163	}
   164	
   165	_db_name_from_url() {
   166	    local url_no_query="${1%%\?*}"
   167	    local db_name="${url_no_query##*/}"
   168	    [ -n "$db_name" ] && [ "$db_name" != "$url_no_query" ] || \
   169	        err "Could not parse database name from configured PostgreSQL URL"
   170	    printf '%s\n' "$db_name"
   171	}
   172	
   173	_clone_url_for_db() {
   174	    local url="$1"
   175	    local clone_db="$2"
   176	    local base="${url%%\?*}"
   177	    local query=""
   178	    if [ "$base" != "$url" ]; then
   179	        query="?${url#*\?}"
   180	    fi
   181	    printf '%s/%s%s\n' "${base%/*}" "$clone_db" "$query"
   182	}
   183	
   184	_run_alembic_apply() {
   185	    if ! docker compose -f "$COMPOSE_FILE" run --rm --no-deps \
   186	            -e PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 -w /app/backend \
   187	            backend-a python -m alembic upgrade heads; then
   188	        err "Alembic upgrade heads 失敗 — 中止部署。修正 migration 後重跑 deploy-prod.sh。"
   189	    fi
   190	    log "Alembic upgrade heads 完成"
   191	}
   192	
   193	_run_alembic_pg_clone() {
   194	    local live_url live_db clone_db clone_url
   195	    live_url="$(_prod_database_url)"
   196	    live_db="$(_db_name_from_url "$live_url")"
   197	    clone_db="${OMNISIGHT_ALEMBIC_CLONE_DB:-omnisight_alembic_dryrun_$(date +%Y%m%d%H%M%S)_$$}"
   198	    clone_url="$(_clone_url_for_db "$live_url" "$clone_db")"
   199	
   200	    step "Step 2.5: Alembic dry-validation on PG clone"
   201	    echo "建立 live PG database 的一次性 clone，對 clone 執行 alembic upgrade heads；live DB 不套 migration。"
   202	
   203	    if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
   204	        echo "  [dry-run] docker exec omnisight-pg-primary drop/create/pg_dump/pg_restore clone '$clone_db' from '$live_db'"
   205	        echo "  [dry-run] docker compose -f $COMPOSE_FILE run --rm --no-deps -e PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 -e SQLALCHEMY_URL=<clone-url> -w /app/backend backend-a python -m alembic upgrade heads"
   206	        echo "  [dry-run] docker exec omnisight-pg-primary dropdb clone '$clone_db'"
   207	        return 0
   208	    fi
   209	
   210	    docker inspect omnisight-pg-primary >/dev/null 2>&1 || \
   211	        err "omnisight-pg-primary container not found; cannot create PG clone for Alembic validation."
   212	
   213	    docker exec -e CLONE_DB="$clone_db" -e LIVE_DB="$live_db" omnisight-pg-primary sh -lc '
   214	        set -eu
   215	        dropdb --if-exists -U "$POSTGRES_USER" "$CLONE_DB"
   216	        createdb -U "$POSTGRES_USER" "$CLONE_DB"
   217	        pg_dump -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$LIVE_DB" -Fc | pg_restore -U "$POSTGRES_USER" -d "$CLONE_DB" --no-owner
   218	    ' || err "PG clone 建立失敗 — live DB 未修改。"
   219	
   220	    if ! docker compose -f "$COMPOSE_FILE" run --rm --no-deps \
   221	            -e PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 \
   222	            -e SQLALCHEMY_URL="$clone_url" \
   223	            -w /app/backend \
   224	            backend-a python -m alembic upgrade heads; then
   225	        docker exec -e CLONE_DB="$clone_db" omnisight-pg-primary sh -lc \
   226	            'dropdb --if-exists -U "$POSTGRES_USER" "$CLONE_DB"' >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   227	        err "Alembic PG clone dry-validation 失敗 — live DB 未修改。"
   228	    fi
   229	
   230	    docker exec -e CLONE_DB="$clone_db" omnisight-pg-primary sh -lc \
   231	        'dropdb --if-exists -U "$POSTGRES_USER" "$CLONE_DB"' || \
   232	        warn "PG clone '$clone_db' cleanup failed; drop it manually after inspection."
   233	    log "Alembic PG clone dry-validation 完成（live DB 未修改）"
   234	}
   235	
   236	step "OmniSight Production 零停機部署"
   237	echo "Compose: $COMPOSE_FILE"
   238	echo "Deploy:  ${TAG:-$DIGEST}"
   239	echo "Alembic: $ALEMBIC_MODE"
   240	echo ""
   241	
   242	# ── Step 1: Pull latest code ──
   243	step "Step 1: 拉取最新程式碼"
   244	
   245	# RT-07a: gate the deploy identity via check_deploy_ref.sh before
   246	# touching the working tree or any replica. The verifier enforces the
   247	# release-train contract — final tag (vX.Y.Z) or image digest only,
   248	# never a branch. `--dry-run` runs the shape gate (+ allowlist for tags)
   249	# without needing the ref fetched locally. There is no insecure bypass.
   250	verify_args=()
   251	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = true ]; then
   252	    verify_args+=("--allowlist-only")
   253	fi
   254	
   255	if [ -n "$DIGEST" ]; then
   256	    # Digest deploy: the image is already built and cosign-verified at
   257	    # this digest (RT-06), so there is no git ref to fetch or check out.
   258	    # Force --skip-build — the image carries the code + Alembic
   259	    # migrations (the alembic step below runs inside that image, not the
   260	    # working tree). Compose pull-by-digest via OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST is
   261	    # wired in RT-07b; here we record the digest as the deploy identity.
   262	    scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind digest --ref "$DIGEST" "${verify_args[@]}"
   263	    SKIP_BUILD=true
   264	    _upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_DIGEST" "$DIGEST"
   265	    log "Digest deploy: $DIGEST (skip-build；不做 git checkout)"
   266	else
   267	    GERRIT_SOURCE="$(_detect_gerrit_source)"
   268	    echo "Git source: $GERRIT_SOURCE"
   269	    _run_cmd git fetch "$GERRIT_SOURCE" --tags
   270	    scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh --kind tag --ref "$TAG" "${verify_args[@]}"
   271	    _run_cmd git checkout "$TAG"
   272	    log "Code 更新完成：$(git log --oneline -1)"
   273	fi
   274	
   275	# OP-772: expose current/previous image tags to the persistent SLO monitor
   276	# before any replica is restarted. The monitor uses the previous tag as
   277	# its rollback target if three consecutive 30 s SLO windows breach.
   278	CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG="${OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG:-${TAG:-$DIGEST}}"
   279	PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG="$(grep -E '^OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=' .env 2>/dev/null | tail -1 | cut -d= -f2- || true)"
   280	if [ -n "${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG:-}" ] && [ "$PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG" != "$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG" ]; then
   281	    _upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG" "$PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG"
   282	fi
   283	_upsert_env "OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG" "$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG"
   284	log "SLO monitor image tags: current=$CURRENT_IMAGE_TAG previous=${PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG:-unknown}"
   285	
   286	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = false ]; then
   287	    if systemctl --user list-unit-files omnisight-slo-monitor.service >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   288	        systemctl --user restart omnisight-slo-monitor.service || \
   289	            warn "omnisight-slo-monitor.service restart failed before deploy — check systemd user logs"
   290	    else
   291	        warn "omnisight-slo-monitor.service not installed — install deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service for OP-772"
   292	    fi
   293	else
   294	    echo "  [dry-run] systemctl --user restart omnisight-slo-monitor.service"
   295	fi
   296	
   297	# ── Step 1b: WAL-safe pre-deploy backup ──
   298	# H2 audit (2026-04-19): rolling deploys can still roll BACKWARDS in
   299	# data integrity if a migration blows up or a code change panics on
   300	# existing rows. The `scripts/backup_prod_db.sh` helper takes a WAL-
   301	# safe online snapshot + optional AES-256-GCM encryption (when
   302	# OMNISIGHT_BACKUP_PASSPHRASE is set). Skipped in --dry-run.
   303	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = false ]; then
   304	    step "Step 1b: Pre-deploy backup"
   305	    if [ -x scripts/backup_prod_db.sh ]; then
   306	        scripts/backup_prod_db.sh --label pre-deploy --prune 20 || \
   307	            err "pre-deploy backup failed — aborting to protect data. Re-run after investigating."
   308	    else
   309	        warn "scripts/backup_prod_db.sh missing — proceeding WITHOUT backup"
   310	    fi
   311	fi
   312	
   313	# ── Step 2: Build (optional) ──
   314	if [ "$SKIP_BUILD" = false ]; then
   315	    step "Step 2: Build Docker images"
   316	    _run "docker compose -f $COMPOSE_FILE build"
   317	    log "Build 完成"
   318	else
   319	    step "Step 2: Skip build (--skip-build)"
   320	    log "使用現有 image"
   321	fi
   322	
   323	# ── Step 2.5: Alembic migrations against live DB ──
   324	# FX.9.5 (2026-05-04): apply pending Alembic migrations BEFORE the
   325	# rolling restart so the new backend replica's /readyz doesn't fail on
   326	# schema drift. Spawns an ephemeral container from the freshly-built
   327	# backend image via `docker compose run --rm --no-deps` — backend-a /
   328	# backend-b stay on the OLD image until Step 3 / Step 4 restart them,
   329	# so the live request path is unaffected during migration.
   330	#
   331	# Knobs mirror bootstrap_prod.sh §4:
   332	#   PYTHONSAFEPATH=1 — defence-in-depth against any future top-level
   333	#                     project module shadowing a stdlib module under
   334	#                     the alembic CLI's import order.
   335	#   -w /app/backend  — alembic.ini's `script_location = alembic` is
   336	#                     relative to invocation CWD (alembic quirk), not
   337	#                     to the ini's own directory.
   338	#   `upgrade heads` (plural) — defensive: works whether the tree is
   339	#                     single-head (post-FX.9.4 merge) or transiently
   340	#                     multi-head (e.g. mid-merge concurrent feature
   341	#                     branches). `head` (singular) would bail with
   342	#                     `Multiple head revisions are present`.
   343	#
   344	# `--no-deps` skips backend-a's `depends_on: docker-socket-proxy` —
   345	# the migration container only needs PG (reachable via the
   346	# `db_ha` external network attached to backend-a's service def);
   347	# the docker-socket-proxy gate is a runtime concern for the long-
   348	# lived replica, not for a one-shot alembic invocation.
   349	#
   350	# Failure semantics: if alembic exits non-zero we abort the deploy
   351	# BEFORE touching either replica. The DB is left in whatever partial
   352	# state alembic reached (Alembic wraps each migration in a tx so a
   353	# single migration is atomic; a multi-migration batch may stop part-
   354	# way and resume on the next run). Operator re-runs `deploy-prod.sh`
   355	# after fixing the bad migration.
   356	if [ "$ALEMBIC_MODE" = "apply" ]; then
   357	    step "Step 2.5: Alembic upgrade heads"
   358	    echo "在 rolling restart 之前用新 image 套 schema（FX.9.5 — 避免 readyz fail）..."
   359	fi
   360	
   361	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = false ]; then
   362	    case "$ALEMBIC_MODE" in
   363	        apply) _run_alembic_apply ;;
   364	        pg-clone) _run_alembic_pg_clone ;;
   365	    esac
   366	else
   367	    case "$ALEMBIC_MODE" in
   368	        apply) echo "  [dry-run] docker compose -f $COMPOSE_FILE run --rm --no-deps backend-a python -m alembic upgrade heads" ;;
   369	        pg-clone) _run_alembic_pg_clone ;;
   370	    esac
   371	fi
   372	
   373	# ── Step 3: Rolling restart backend-a ──
   374	step "Step 3: Rolling restart — backend-a"
   375	echo "Caddy 會自動將流量切到 backend-b..."
   376	
   377	_run "docker compose -f $COMPOSE_FILE up -d --no-deps backend-a"
   378	
   379	echo -n "等待 backend-a readyz..."
   380	if [ "$DRY_RUN" = false ]; then

   650	
   651	class _InlineScriptCollector(HTMLParser):
   652	    def __init__(self) -> None:
   653	        super().__init__()
   654	        self.sources: list[str] = []
   655	        self._in_inline_script = False
   656	        self._parts: list[str] = []
   657	
   658	    def handle_starttag(self, tag: str, attrs) -> None:
   659	        if tag.lower() != "script":
   660	            return
   661	        attr_names = {name.lower() for name, _ in attrs}
   662	        if "src" in attr_names:
   663	            return
   664	        self._in_inline_script = True
   665	        self._parts = []
   666	
   667	    def handle_data(self, data: str) -> None:
   668	        if self._in_inline_script:
   669	            self._parts.append(data)
   670	
   671	    def handle_endtag(self, tag: str) -> None:
   672	        if tag.lower() != "script" or not self._in_inline_script:
   673	            return
   674	        source = "".join(self._parts)
   675	        if source:
   676	            self.sources.append(source)
   677	        self._in_inline_script = False
   678	        self._parts = []
   679	
   680	
   681	def _generate_csp_nonce() -> str:
   682	    """Generate a per-request CSP nonce from the kernel CSPRNG."""
   683	    return base64.b64encode(secrets.token_bytes(_CSP_NONCE_RAW_BYTES)).decode("ascii")
   684	
   685	
   686	def _csp_sha256_source(source: str | bytes) -> str:
   687	    """Return a CSP ``sha256-...`` source expression for inline content."""
   688	    raw = source if isinstance(source, bytes) else source.encode("utf-8")
   689	    digest = base64.b64encode(hashlib.sha256(raw).digest()).decode("ascii")
   690	    return f"'sha256-{digest}'"
   691	
   692	
   693	def _build_content_security_policy(nonce: str, hash_sources) -> str:
   694	    script_sources = ["script-src 'self'", f"'nonce-{nonce}'"]
   695	    for source in hash_sources or ():
   696	        script_sources.append(_csp_sha256_source(source))
   697	    return "; ".join((*_CSP_BASE_DIRECTIVES, " ".join(script_sources)))
   698	
   699	
   700	def _html_charset(content_type: str) -> str:
   701	    match = _CONTENT_TYPE_CHARSET_RE.search(content_type or "")
   702	    return match.group(1) if match else "utf-8"
   703	
   704	
   705	def _inline_script_hash_sources(body: bytes, content_type: str) -> list[str]:
   706	    if "text/html" not in (content_type or "").lower():
   707	        return []
   708	    try:
   709	        html_text = body.decode(_html_charset(content_type), errors="strict")
   710	    except (LookupError, UnicodeDecodeError):
   711	        return []
   712	    collector = _InlineScriptCollector()
   713	    collector.feed(html_text)
   714	    return collector.sources

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""G7 (HA-07) — helpers that drive the four HA-observability metrics.
     2	
     3	This module sits behind the Prometheus metric primitives in
     4	``backend/metrics.py``: it knows *when* to set / increment the gauges
     5	and counters and computes the in-process rolling 5xx rate that we
     6	export as a convenience gauge.
     7	
     8	Responsibilities
     9	----------------
    10	
    11	1. **instance_up** — a 1/0 gauge (per replica) set to 1 the moment
    12	   the Starlette app boots and cleared to 0 when the lifecycle
    13	   coordinator flips to draining.
    14	2. **rolling_deploy 5xx tracking** — a Starlette middleware observes
    15	   every HTTP response, increments the per-status-class counter, and
    16	   updates an in-memory 60 s ring-buffer from which the rolling 5xx
    17	   share (0..1) is published as ``omnisight_rolling_deploy_5xx_rate``.
    18	3. **replica_lag_seconds** — a thin setter a pg_ha sampler can call.
    19	4. **readyz_latency_seconds** — a context manager the /readyz handler
    20	   wraps its body in.
    21	
    22	Everything here is deliberately side-effect-free at import time so
    23	tests can exercise individual pieces without booting the whole app.
    24	
    25	Contract: when prometheus_client is absent the metrics are `_NoOp`
    26	stubs and every function here still returns sensibly (zeros, no
    27	exceptions). That means test environments without prometheus still
    28	run.
    29	"""
    30	
    31	from __future__ import annotations
    32	
    33	import collections
    34	import logging
    35	import os
    36	import socket
    37	import threading
    38	import time
    39	from contextlib import contextmanager
    40	from typing import Iterator
    41	
    42	from backend import metrics as _metrics
    43	
    44	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    45	
    46	
    47	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    48	#  Instance identity
    49	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    50	def get_instance_id() -> str:
    51	    """Return a stable identifier for this backend replica.
    52	
    53	    Resolution order:
    54	      1. ``OMNISIGHT_INSTANCE_ID`` env var — the deployment tool
    55	         (helm / compose) sets this per pod/container.
    56	      2. ``HOSTNAME`` env var — populated by k8s + docker by default.
    57	      3. ``socket.gethostname()`` — last-resort OS fallback.
    58	    """
    59	    for env_name in ("OMNISIGHT_INSTANCE_ID", "HOSTNAME"):
    60	        val = os.environ.get(env_name, "").strip()
    61	        if val:
    62	            return val
    63	    try:
    64	        return socket.gethostname() or "unknown"
    65	    except OSError:
    66	        return "unknown"
    67	
    68	
    69	def mark_instance_up() -> None:
    70	    """Flip ``omnisight_backend_instance_up`` to 1 for this replica."""
    71	    _metrics.backend_instance_up.labels(instance_id=get_instance_id()).set(1)
    72	
    73	
    74	def mark_instance_down() -> None:
    75	    """Flip ``omnisight_backend_instance_up`` to 0 for this replica.
    76	
    77	    Called from the lifecycle coordinator the moment SIGTERM / draining
    78	    begins so the reverse-proxy / k8s service removes the pod from
    79	    rotation.
    80	    """
    81	    _metrics.backend_instance_up.labels(instance_id=get_instance_id()).set(0)
    82	
    83	
    84	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    85	#  Rolling 5xx tracker
    86	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    87	# 60-second rolling window, 1-second resolution. Two deques of equal
    88	# length — one for 5xx counts, one for total counts — keyed by the
    89	# integer epoch second. On every observation we advance the buckets
    90	# and accumulate.
    91	
    92	_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60
    93	_lock = threading.Lock()
    94	_total_bucket: collections.deque[tuple[int, int]] = collections.deque()
    95	_fivexx_bucket: collections.deque[tuple[int, int]] = collections.deque()
    96	
    97	
    98	def _status_class(status_code: int) -> str:
    99	    """Map an HTTP status code to the label value '2xx'..'5xx'.
   100	
   101	    Status codes outside the 100-599 range are bucketed as '5xx' —
   102	    they shouldn't happen, but if they do they're clearly a bug the
   103	    operator wants to see in the error counter rather than silently
   104	    dropped.
   105	    """
   106	    if 200 <= status_code < 300:
   107	        return "2xx"
   108	    if 300 <= status_code < 400:
   109	        return "3xx"
   110	    if 400 <= status_code < 500:
   111	        return "4xx"
   112	    return "5xx"
   113	
   114	
   115	def _prune_locked(now_s: int) -> None:
   116	    """Drop samples older than _WINDOW_SECONDS. Caller holds _lock."""
   117	    cutoff = now_s - _WINDOW_SECONDS
   118	    while _total_bucket and _total_bucket[0][0] <= cutoff:
   119	        _total_bucket.popleft()
   120	    while _fivexx_bucket and _fivexx_bucket[0][0] <= cutoff:
   121	        _fivexx_bucket.popleft()
   122	
   123	
   124	def record_http_response(status_code: int, *, now: float | None = None) -> None:
   125	    """Bump the per-status-class counter + slide the 5xx rolling window.
   126	
   127	    Safe to call from both sync and async contexts (uses a plain
   128	    ``threading.Lock``). The ``now`` parameter exists for tests that
   129	    want to pin the wall-clock time.
   130	    """
   131	    # Counter bump — always; unconditionally exported.
   132	    cls = _status_class(status_code)
   133	    _metrics.rolling_deploy_responses_total.labels(status_class=cls).inc()
   134	
   135	    # Rolling window — only tracks 5xx share vs. total.
   136	    now_s = int(now if now is not None else time.time())
   137	    with _lock:
   138	        _prune_locked(now_s)
   139	        # Append or coalesce into the current-second bucket.
   140	        if _total_bucket and _total_bucket[-1][0] == now_s:
   141	            _total_bucket[-1] = (now_s, _total_bucket[-1][1] + 1)
   142	        else:
   143	            _total_bucket.append((now_s, 1))
   144	        if cls == "5xx":
   145	            if _fivexx_bucket and _fivexx_bucket[-1][0] == now_s:
   146	                _fivexx_bucket[-1] = (now_s, _fivexx_bucket[-1][1] + 1)
   147	            else:
   148	                _fivexx_bucket.append((now_s, 1))
   149	        rate = _current_rate_locked()
   150	    _metrics.rolling_deploy_5xx_rate.set(rate)
   151	
   152	
   153	def _current_rate_locked() -> float:
   154	    total = sum(c for _, c in _total_bucket)
   155	    if total == 0:
   156	        return 0.0
   157	    fivexx = sum(c for _, c in _fivexx_bucket)
   158	    return fivexx / total
   159	
   160	
   161	def current_5xx_rate(*, now: float | None = None) -> float:
   162	    """Return the rolling 5xx rate (0..1) without recording a response."""
   163	    now_s = int(now if now is not None else time.time())
   164	    with _lock:
   165	        _prune_locked(now_s)
   166	        rate = _current_rate_locked()
   167	    _metrics.rolling_deploy_5xx_rate.set(rate)
   168	    return rate
   169	
   170	
   171	def reset_rolling_window() -> None:
   172	    """Clear the in-memory rolling window. Used by tests."""
   173	    with _lock:
   174	        _total_bucket.clear()
   175	        _fivexx_bucket.clear()
   176	    _metrics.rolling_deploy_5xx_rate.set(0.0)
   177	
   178	
   179	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   180	#  Replica lag
   181	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   182	def update_replica_lag(replica: str, lag_seconds: float) -> None:
   183	    """Publish the current streaming-replication lag for a standby.
   184	
   185	    The caller is typically a background sampler querying
   186	    ``pg_stat_replication`` every few seconds.
   187	    """
   188	    if lag_seconds < 0:
   189	        # A negative lag is meaningless; clamp to 0 so Grafana stays
   190	        # honest but we don't silently drop the sample.
   191	        lag_seconds = 0.0
   192	    _metrics.replica_lag_seconds.labels(replica=replica).set(lag_seconds)
   193	
   194	
   195	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   196	#  /readyz latency
   197	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   198	@contextmanager
   199	def observe_readyz_latency(outcome: str = "ready") -> Iterator[None]:
   200	    """Context manager that observes the /readyz handler's wall-clock.
   201	
   202	    ``outcome`` must be one of ``ready | not_ready | draining`` — it
   203	    shows up as a label on the histogram so Grafana can split the
   204	    distribution by success/failure class.
   205	    """
   206	    started = time.perf_counter()
   207	    try:
   208	        yield
   209	    finally:
   210	        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - started
   211	        _metrics.readyz_latency_seconds.labels(outcome=outcome).observe(elapsed)
   212	
   213	
   214	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   215	#  Middleware registration
   216	# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   217	def register_middleware(app) -> None:
   218	    """Attach the HTTP middleware that feeds the 5xx rolling window.
   219	
   220	    Called from ``backend/main.py`` once at import time. Kept here so
   221	    tests can build a minimal ASGI app without dragging in the full
   222	    OmniSight bootstrap.
   223	    """
   224	
   225	    @app.middleware("http")
   226	    async def _rolling_deploy_5xx_middleware(request, call_next):
   227	        try:
   228	            response = await call_next(request)
   229	        except Exception:
   230	            # An unhandled exception is a 5xx by contract. Record it
   231	            # then re-raise so Starlette's exception handlers still
   232	            # fire and the client sees the 500 payload it expects.
   233	            record_http_response(500)
   234	            raise
   235	        record_http_response(response.status_code)
   236	        return response

     1	# OP-883 D11 — Continuous SLO Monitor & Auto-Rollback Runbook
     2	
     3	**Ticket:** OP-883 (D11, META OP-761 Sprint D §Phase 3) • **Status:** active
     4	**Module:** `backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py`
     5	**Config:** `config/slo_thresholds.yaml`
     6	**Related runbooks:** `docs/operations/slo-monitor-rollback.md` (OP-772
     7	deploy-window worker, narrower scope) • `docs/operations/prod-deploy-runbook.md`
     8	(OP-881 D9 deploy orchestrator) • `docs/operations/as-rollout-and-rollback.md`
     9	(OP-771 D10 canary controller).
    10	
    11	---
    12	
    13	## 1. What this monitor does
    14	
    15	The D11 monitor is the **always-on** SLO watchdog above the production
    16	stack. Every 30 s it samples Prometheus and asks two questions:
    17	
    18	1. Is the **error rate** above 1% over the last minute? (AC #2)
    19	2. Is the **p95 latency** above 500 ms over the last 5 minutes? (AC #2)
    20	
    21	If either answer stays "yes" for **more than 2 minutes** (AC #3), the
    22	monitor:
    23	
    24	1. publishes an SSE frame `slo.breach` carrying the breached metric(s),
    25	   the live numbers, and the rollback mode (`canary` or `full`);
    26	2. pages the on-call operator (`NotificationLevel.critical`, severity
    27	   `P1`, source `slo-monitor`);
    28	3. invokes the rollback trigger — **D10 canary abort** when a canary
    29	   rollout is currently `running` or `paused`, **D9 full prod
    30	   rollback** otherwise;
    31	4. arms a 10-min cooldown (AC #4) during which further ticks short-
    32	   circuit before touching Prometheus.
    33	
    34	When the metric backend itself goes dark the monitor **fails open**:
    35	it logs a warning and refuses to auto-rollback. A Prometheus outage
    36	is not evidence of a prod incident, and one of D11's hard rules is
    37	that the measurement plane cannot trigger the control plane.
    38	
    39	## 2. SLO thresholds (config/slo_thresholds.yaml)
    40	
    41	| Key                          | Default | Spec |
    42	|------------------------------|--------:|------|
    43	| `error_rate_max`             |   0.01  | AC #2 — error rate < 1% |
    44	| `p95_latency_ms_max`         |   500   | AC #2 — p95 < 500ms     |
    45	| `error_rate_window_seconds`  |    60   | 1-min window            |
    46	| `p95_window_seconds`         |   300   | 5-min window            |
    47	| `sample_interval_seconds`    |    30   | AC #1 — sample every 30s|
    48	| `breach_sustain_seconds`     |   120   | AC #3 — >2min sustain    |
    49	| `cooldown_seconds`           |   600   | AC #4 — 10-min cooldown  |
    50	
    51	Edit the YAML, then `systemctl restart omnisight-slo-monitor` to pick
    52	up the new numbers. Partial YAML is supported: any unspecified key
    53	falls back to its dataclass default.
    54	
    55	## 3. Operator override — suppress flag (AC #5)
    56	
    57	For **known transient** incidents (planned partner outage, scheduled
    58	chaos drill, third-party CDN cutover) the monitor must not fight the
    59	operator. Toggle the suppress flag two ways — the monitor re-reads
    60	both every tick, no restart needed:
    61	
    62	```bash
    63	# File-based (preferred — survives daemon restart):
    64	touch deploy/blue-green/slo_monitor_suppress.flag
    65	# … incident resolved …
    66	rm  deploy/blue-green/slo_monitor_suppress.flag
    67	```
    68	
    69	```bash
    70	# Or env-based (process-scoped, useful in tmux for short windows):
    71	export OMNISIGHT_SLO_MONITOR_SUPPRESS=1
    72	```
    73	
    74	While suppressed, every tick returns `suppressed` and the in-flight
    75	breach streak is cleared. Removing the flag mid-incident does **not**
    76	instantly retrigger — the 2-min sustain window restarts from scratch,
    77	giving the operator time to verify the prod stack is actually healthy
    78	before the auto-rollback re-arms.
    79	
    80	## 4. SSE frame schema (frontend contract)
    81	
    82	```
    83	event: slo.breach
    84	data: {
    85	  "error_rate":               0.05,
    86	  "p95_latency_ms":           120.0,
    87	  "error_rate_threshold":     0.01,
    88	  "p95_latency_ms_threshold": 500.0,
    89	  "breached_metrics":         ["error_rate"],
    90	  "rollback_mode":            "canary",
    91	  "reason":                   "SLO breach sustained >120s on error_rate",
    92	  "timestamp":                "2026-05-11T…Z"
    93	}
    94	```
    95	
    96	The frontend renders this as a red-card incident banner on the
    97	deploy dashboard. The `rollback_mode` field decides whether the
    98	banner links to the canary controller or the prod-deploy audit row.
    99	
   100	## 5. Rollback decision table
   101	
   102	| Canary state (`deploy/blue-green/canary_state.json`) | Mode    | What fires |
   103	|------------------------------------------------------|---------|------------|
   104	| `running` or `paused`                                | canary  | `CanaryController.manual_control(state, "abort")` — Caddy snippet drops back to the stable upstream. |
   105	| `aborted` / `completed` / file missing               | full    | `ProductionDeployOrchestrator._rollback(<current tag>)` — i.e. `scripts/deploy.sh --rollback`. |
   106	
   107	The mode is picked at trigger time, not at monitor start, so a
   108	canary that completes mid-incident transitions cleanly to the D9
   109	full path on the next sustained breach.
   110	
   111	## 6. Failure modes
   112	
   113	| Symptom                                  | Monitor behaviour                                                              | Operator action |
   114	|------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------|------------------|
   115	| Prometheus 5xx / timeout                 | logs `metric source unavailable`, returns `metric_unavailable`, no rollback    | Investigate the metric backend; do **not** disable the monitor — fail-open is by design. |
   116	| Rollback trigger raises                  | logs the exception, returns `RollbackOutcome(status="failed", …)`              | Run the rollback manually per the canary / prod runbook; check the on-call page. |
   117	| Cooldown active during manual rollback   | `maybe_rollback()` raises `CooldownInEffect`                                   | Wait out the 10-min window or call `set_engine_for_tests` from a shell — DO NOT bypass in prod. |
   118	| Flag file present but env unset (or vice-versa) | `is_suppressed()` returns `True` (either source wins)                    | Both sources are intentional; document which one you flipped in the incident log. |
   119	
   120	## 7. Manual rollback (operator-driven)
   121	
   122	When the auto path is unsafe (e.g. a region-specific incident the
   123	monitor cannot see) drop to the shell:
   124	
   125	```python
   126	from backend.orchestrator import slo_monitor as sm
   127	monitor = sm.build_default_monitor()
   128	outcome = monitor.maybe_rollback("region-eu-1 packet loss, manual drain")
   129	print(outcome)  # RollbackOutcome(mode=…, status=…, detail=…)
   130	```
   131	
   132	`maybe_rollback` honours the AC #4 cooldown — if the auto-monitor
   133	already fired in the last 10 minutes it raises `CooldownInEffect`
   134	rather than racing the auto path. Wait it out and retry.
   135	
   136	## 8. Synthetic-breach DoD on staging
   137	
   138	The DoD calls for one synthetic breach on staging that triggers a
   139	verified auto-rollback. Drive it via:
   140	
   141	```bash
   142	# Stage 1: arm the suppress flag so a real breach can't fire
   143	# while we set up.
   144	touch deploy/blue-green/slo_monitor_suppress.flag
   145	
   146	# Stage 2: in another shell, point a load generator at the staging
   147	# mirror with a 5xx-injection knob (e.g. vegeta + ENABLE_FAULT_5XX=1).
   148	# Confirm Prometheus shows error_rate >= 0.05 on a 1-min window.
   149	
   150	# Stage 3: drop the flag. Within breach_sustain_seconds + sample_interval
   151	# the monitor should emit `slo.breach` and call the canary abort.
   152	rm deploy/blue-green/slo_monitor_suppress.flag
   153	
   154	# Stage 4: verify
   155	#   * `deploy/blue-green/canary_state.json` is now `aborted`
   156	#   * an SSE `slo.breach` frame reached the dashboard
   157	#   * the on-call PagerDuty received the P1
   158	```
   159	
   160	Sign off the drill in the OP-883 ticket comment with the timestamp of
   161	the breach SSE and the rollback outcome.
     1	# OP-772 SLO Monitor + Auto-Rollback
     2	
     3	Production deploys use `config/slos.yaml` as the SLO source of truth:
     4	
     5	- error rate `< 0.5%`
     6	- p95 latency `< 500 ms`
     7	- success rate `> 99.5%`
     8	- monthly error budget `1 h`
     9	
    10	`deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service` runs `python -m backend.slo_monitor`
    11	as a persistent worker. The worker samples Prometheus every 30 seconds and
    12	keeps running through the deploy and for the post-deploy hour. A breach is
    13	three consecutive 30-second windows above threshold for the same route metric.
    14	
    15	When a breach fires, the monitor posts a medium operator notification, checks
    16	that the previous backend and frontend image tags exist in GHCR, and then runs:
    17	
    18	```bash
    19	OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG=<previous-tag> \
    20	docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d --no-deps backend-a backend-b frontend
    21	```
    22	
    23	Rollback is bounded by `rollback_max_seconds: 300`. If either previous image is
    24	missing from the registry, the monitor posts a high-severity alert and halts
    25	without changing the running services; the operator must intervene manually.

   254	class ProductionDeployOrchestrator:
   255	    def __init__(
   256	        self,
   257	        *,
   258	        runner: CommandRunner = run_command,
   259	        compose_file: str = "docker-compose.prod.yml",
   260	        image_repository: str | None = None,
   261	        staging_rollback_command: list[str] | None = None,
   262	        health_timeout_seconds: float = 120.0,
   263	        deploy_timeout_seconds: float = 600.0,
   264	        clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic,
   265	    ) -> None:
   266	        self.runner = runner
   267	        self.compose_file = compose_file
   268	        self.image_repository = (
   269	            image_repository if image_repository is not None else _default_image_repository()
   270	        )
   271	        raw_cancel = os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_RELEASE_STAGING_ROLLBACK_CMD", "").strip()
   272	        self.staging_rollback_command = (
   273	            list(staging_rollback_command)
   274	            if staging_rollback_command is not None
   275	            else (shlex.split(raw_cancel) if raw_cancel else ["true"])
   276	        )
   277	        self.health_timeout_seconds = health_timeout_seconds
   278	        self.deploy_timeout_seconds = deploy_timeout_seconds
   279	        self.clock = clock
   280	
   281	    def _run(self, args: list[str], *, timeout: float, tag: str) -> None:
   282	        env = os.environ.copy()
   283	        env["OMNISIGHT_RELEASE_IMAGE_TAG"] = tag
   284	        self.runner(args, timeout=timeout, env=env)
   285	
   286	    def _rollback(self, tag: str) -> None:
   287	        self._run(["scripts/deploy.sh", "--rollback"], timeout=120.0, tag=tag)
   288	
   289	    def ship(self, tag: str) -> float:
   290	        started = self.clock()
   291	        try:
   292	            self._run(["docker", "pull", f"{self.image_repository}:{tag}"], timeout=300.0, tag=tag)
   293	            self._run(
   294	                [
   295	                    "docker", "compose", "-f", self.compose_file, "run", "--rm",
   296	                    "--no-deps", "-e", "PYTHONSAFEPATH=1", "-w", "/app/backend",
   297	                    "backend-a", "python", "-m", "alembic", "upgrade", "head",
   298	                ],
   299	                timeout=300.0,
   300	                tag=tag,
   301	            )
   302	            for service, port in (("backend-a", "8000"), ("backend-b", "8001")):
   303	                self._run(
   304	                    ["docker", "compose", "-f", self.compose_file, "up", "-d", "--no-deps", service],
   305	                    timeout=120.0,
   306	                    tag=tag,
   307	                )
   308	                self._run(
   309	                    ["curl", "-sf", f"http://localhost:{port}/readyz"],
   310	                    timeout=self.health_timeout_seconds,
   311	                    tag=tag,
   312	                )
   313	            self._run(
   314	                ["docker", "compose", "-f", self.compose_file, "up", "-d", "--no-deps", "frontend"],
   315	                timeout=120.0,
   316	                tag=tag,
   317	            )
   318	        except Exception:
   319	            self._rollback(tag)
   320	            raise
   321	        elapsed = self.clock() - started
   322	        if elapsed >= self.deploy_timeout_seconds:
   323	            self._rollback(tag)
   324	            raise TimeoutError(f"production deploy exceeded {self.deploy_timeout_seconds:.0f}s")
   325	        return elapsed
   326	
   327	    def cancel(self, tag: str) -> None:
   328	        self._run(self.staging_rollback_command, timeout=120.0, tag=tag)
   329	
   330	
   331	def _require_reason(reason: str | None, action: str) -> str:
   332	    """OP-779 D18 -- prod approval workflow must capture an operator reason."""
   160	# `--rollback` deliberately short-circuits the ENV requirement, the git
   161	# checkout, the db backup, the pip/pnpm build, and the strategy
   162	# dispatch. The previous color's container was kept warm by row 1355's
   163	# ceremony precisely so rollback is a single-rename(2) symlink flip —
   164	# measured in seconds, not minutes. Putting this block BEFORE the
   165	# `ENV` check is what lets operators type `scripts/deploy.sh --rollback`
   166	# with no other args at 3am.
   167	if [[ "$ROLLBACK_FLAG" == "1" ]]; then
   168	  ROOT=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)
   169	  cd "$ROOT"
   170	
   171	  rblog() { printf '\033[35m[rollback]\033[0m %s\n' "$*"; }
   172	
   173	  # Honour OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DIR the same way bluegreen_switch.sh does
   174	  # so contract tests can sandbox the state dir without mutating the
   175	  # committed repo. Default is $ROOT/deploy/blue-green (production).
   176	  BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR="${OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DIR:-$ROOT/deploy/blue-green}"
   177	  BLUEGREEN_SWITCH="$ROOT/scripts/bluegreen_switch.sh"
   178	
   179	  # (a) Primitive present?
   180	  if [[ ! -x "$BLUEGREEN_SWITCH" || ! -d "$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR" ]]; then
   181	    echo "[rollback] blue-green primitive missing (expected $BLUEGREEN_SWITCH + $BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR) — nothing to roll back (has the G3 ceremony ever run on this host?)" >&2
   182	    exit 5
   183	  fi
   184	
   185	  RB_ACTIVE_FILE="$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/active_color"
   186	  RB_PREV_FILE="$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/previous_color"
   187	  RB_RETENTION_FILE="$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/previous_retention_until"
   188	
   189	  # (b) previous_color breadcrumb?
   190	  if [[ ! -f "$RB_PREV_FILE" ]]; then
   191	    echo "[rollback] no previous_color recorded at $RB_PREV_FILE — there is no prior color to roll back to (has a cutover happened on this host?)" >&2
   192	    exit 2
   193	  fi
   194	  RB_PREV_COLOR=$(tr -d '[:space:]' < "$RB_PREV_FILE")
   195	  if [[ "$RB_PREV_COLOR" != "blue" && "$RB_PREV_COLOR" != "green" ]]; then
   196	    echo "[rollback] invalid previous_color '$RB_PREV_COLOR' in $RB_PREV_FILE (expected blue|green)" >&2
   197	    exit 2
   198	  fi
   199	
   200	  if [[ ! -f "$RB_ACTIVE_FILE" ]]; then
   201	    echo "[rollback] no active_color state at $RB_ACTIVE_FILE — state dir looks uninitialised" >&2
   202	    exit 5
   203	  fi
   204	  RB_CURR_COLOR=$(tr -d '[:space:]' < "$RB_ACTIVE_FILE")
   205	
   206	  # No-op guard: if active already equals previous (e.g. two rollbacks
   207	  # in quick succession without an intervening cutover), we're already
   208	  # where we'd flip to. Bail cleanly rather than ping-pong.
   209	  if [[ "$RB_CURR_COLOR" == "$RB_PREV_COLOR" ]]; then
   210	    rblog "already on $RB_CURR_COLOR (previous_color=$RB_PREV_COLOR) — nothing to roll back (no-op)"
   211	    exit 0
   212	  fi
   213	
   214	  # Map color → host port (mirrors blue-green arm + G2 compose topology).
   215	  case "$RB_PREV_COLOR" in
   216	    blue)  RB_PREV_PORT=8000 ;;
   217	    green) RB_PREV_PORT=8001 ;;
   218	    *)     echo "[rollback] unknown color '$RB_PREV_COLOR'" >&2; exit 2 ;;
   219	  esac
   220	
   221	  rblog "plan: $RB_CURR_COLOR → $RB_PREV_COLOR (previous host port :$RB_PREV_PORT)"
   222	
   223	  # (c) Retention window. 24 h default (row 1355 breadcrumb). Bypass
   224	  # via OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_FORCE=1 for the rare case where an operator
   225	  # wants to try anyway (e.g. after manually recreating the old color).
   226	  if [[ -f "$RB_RETENTION_FILE" ]]; then
   227	    RB_RETENTION_UNTIL=$(tr -d '[:space:]' < "$RB_RETENTION_FILE")
   228	    RB_NOW=$(date +%s)
   229	    if [[ "$RB_RETENTION_UNTIL" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && (( RB_NOW > RB_RETENTION_UNTIL )); then
   230	      if [[ "${OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_FORCE:-0}" != "1" ]]; then
   231	        RB_RETENTION_ISO=$(date -u -d "@$RB_RETENTION_UNTIL" '+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ' 2>/dev/null \
   232	          || python3 -c "import datetime,sys; print(datetime.datetime.utcfromtimestamp(int(sys.argv[1])).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'))" "$RB_RETENTION_UNTIL")
   233	        echo "[rollback] retention window EXPIRED (ended $RB_RETENTION_ISO, now=$RB_NOW) — previous color ($RB_PREV_COLOR) may have been pruned. Rolling back blind would point Caddy at a dead upstream." >&2
   234	        echo "[rollback]   bypass: OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_FORCE=1 (DANGEROUS — only if you verified $RB_PREV_COLOR is still live)" >&2
   235	        exit 8
   236	      fi
   237	      echo "[rollback] WARN: retention window expired but OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_FORCE=1 is set — proceeding at operator's risk" >&2
   238	    fi
   239	  else
   240	    echo "[rollback] WARN: no previous_retention_until breadcrumb at $RB_RETENTION_FILE — proceeding without retention gate (pre-row-1355 state dir?)" >&2
   241	  fi
   242	
   243	  # Dry-run exit point — everything above is read-only state inspection;
   244	  # from here on we'd actually flip the symlink + write breadcrumbs.
   245	  if [[ "${OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DRY_RUN:-0}" == "1" ]]; then
   246	    rblog "OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DRY_RUN=1 — plan printed, no symlink / Caddy changes"
   247	    exit 0
   248	  fi
   249	
   250	  # (d) /readyz pre-flight — we refuse to flip to a dead upstream.
   251	  # The whole point of the 24 h warm-standby is instant rollback; if
   252	  # the container isn't responding, flipping the symlink would just
   253	  # shift the outage to the "rolled back" pretense. Bypass is for the
   254	  # rare case where the operator is about to `docker compose up
   255	  # backend-<prev>` right after this runs.
   256	  if [[ "${OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_SKIP_PREFLIGHT:-0}" != "1" ]]; then
   257	    rb_ready_url="http://localhost:${RB_PREV_PORT}/readyz"
   258	    if ! curl -sf -m 5 "$rb_ready_url" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
   259	      rb_prev_svc="backend-a"; [[ "$RB_PREV_COLOR" == "green" ]] && rb_prev_svc="backend-b"
   260	      echo "[rollback] previous color ($RB_PREV_COLOR :$RB_PREV_PORT) /readyz NOT responding — rolling back would point Caddy at a dead upstream." >&2
   261	      echo "[rollback]   triage: docker compose -f docker-compose.prod.yml logs --tail=200 $rb_prev_svc" >&2
   262	      echo "[rollback]   bypass: OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_SKIP_PREFLIGHT=1 (DANGEROUS — only if you're about to recreate $rb_prev_svc manually)" >&2
   263	      exit 3
   264	    fi
   265	    rblog "pre-flight: previous color ($RB_PREV_COLOR :$RB_PREV_PORT) /readyz OK"
   266	  else
   267	    echo "[rollback] WARN: OMNISIGHT_ROLLBACK_SKIP_PREFLIGHT=1 — skipping /readyz pre-flight (DANGEROUS)" >&2
   268	  fi
   269	
   270	  # ── ATOMIC SYMLINK FLIP ───────────────────────────────────────────
   271	  # Delegate to the G3 #2 primitive — rename(2) over the symlink. This
   272	  # is THE cutover (everything above is read-only; everything below is
   273	  # post-flip audit). Passing OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DIR through so the
   274	  # primitive targets the same sandbox we just validated.
   275	  rblog "ATOMIC ROLLBACK → $RB_PREV_COLOR (was: $RB_CURR_COLOR)"
   276	  OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_DIR="$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR" "$BLUEGREEN_SWITCH" rollback
   277	
   278	  # Optional Caddy reload — same delegation pattern as the row-1355 arm.
   279	  BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD="${OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD:-}"
   280	  if [[ -n "$BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD" ]]; then
   281	    rblog "reloading Caddy ($BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD)"
   282	    if ! bash -c "$BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD"; then
   283	      echo "[rollback] WARN: Caddy reload command failed — symlink is already on $RB_PREV_COLOR, but the running Caddy may still see the old upstream. Run the reload command by hand." >&2
   284	    fi
   285	  else
   286	    rblog "OMNISIGHT_BLUEGREEN_CADDY_RELOAD_CMD not set — reload Caddy manually (e.g. docker compose exec -T caddy caddy reload --config /etc/caddy/Caddyfile)"
   287	  fi
   288	
   289	  # Audit breadcrumb — row 1357 runbook + any future timeline reconstructor
   290	  # reads this to know when the rollback happened. Same atomic tmp-then-mv
   291	  # pattern as the row-1355 ceremony so a concurrent reader never sees a
   292	  # half-written file.
   293	  rb_ts=$(date +%s)
   294	  printf '%s\n' "$rb_ts" > "$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/rollback_timestamp.tmp.$$"
   295	  mv -f "$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/rollback_timestamp.tmp.$$" "$BLUEGREEN_STATE_DIR/rollback_timestamp"
   296	
   297	  rblog "rollback complete: now active=$RB_PREV_COLOR (:$RB_PREV_PORT). Old color ($RB_CURR_COLOR) still running as new standby — investigate logs before next cutover."
   298	  exit 0
   299	fi
   300	
   301	ENV=${1:-}
   302	GIT_REF=${2:-}
   303	STRATEGY_ARG=${3:-}
   304	
   305	if [[ -z "$ENV" ]]; then

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	# Deploy Prod Runbook
     2	
     3	`scripts/deploy-prod.sh` is the rolling production deploy entrypoint. It
     4	fetches the deploy ref, verifies it with `scripts/check_deploy_ref.sh`,
     5	builds or reuses images, handles Alembic, then restarts backend replicas
     6	one at a time.
     7	
     8	## Git Source
     9	
    10	Production deploys must fetch from Gerrit, not a stale GitHub mirror.
    11	The script auto-detects the source in this order:
    12	
    13	1. `--gerrit-source=<remote>` or `OMNISIGHT_GERRIT_SOURCE=<remote>`.
    14	2. A remote named `gerrit`.
    15	3. Any configured remote whose URL looks like Gerrit (`gerrit`,
    16	   `sora.services`, or SSH port `29418`).
    17	
    18	If no Gerrit source is found, the deploy refuses to continue. To force a
    19	known-good remote:
    20	
    21	```bash
    22	./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --gerrit-source=gerrit
    23	```
    24	
    25	## Alembic Modes
    26	
    27	Default mode applies migrations to the live database before any backend
    28	replica restarts:
    29	
    30	```bash
    31	./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --alembic-mode=apply
    32	```
    33	
    34	For dry validation, use a PostgreSQL clone instead of Alembic `--sql`.
    35	This catches migrations that depend on runtime bind behavior such as
    36	`exec_driver_sql`, without mutating the live DB:
    37	
    38	```bash
    39	./scripts/deploy-prod.sh --alembic-mode=pg-clone
    40	```
    41	
    42	`pg-clone` reads `SQLALCHEMY_URL`, `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`, or
    43	`DATABASE_URL` from the environment or `.env`, creates a temporary
    44	database on `omnisight-pg-primary`, copies the live DB into it with
    45	`pg_dump | pg_restore`, runs `python -m alembic upgrade heads` inside
    46	the backend image against the clone, then drops the clone.
    47	
    48	Use `--dry-run --alembic-mode=pg-clone` to print the clone plan without
    49	creating or dropping any database.
    50	
    51	## Image Tag Propagation
    52	
    53	`OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG` must be visible to Docker Compose and the SLO
    54	monitor. `deploy-prod.sh` computes the current image tag from
    55	`OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG`, `--tag`, or the current commit, then writes these
    56	values into `.env` before restarting services:
    57	
    58	- `OMNISIGHT_IMAGE_TAG`
    59	- `OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG` when the tag changes
    60	
    61	Do not rely on a one-off shell export alone for production deploy state;
    62	the persistent `.env` value is the handoff point consumed by Compose
    63	`env_file` and monitor restarts.
