**Result: audit-needs-revision**, but the core risk is real.

The audit is sound on G2/G4 as shipped behavior, but it overstates G3, underspecifies G5, and misses one deployment-path blocker: the shipped systemd unit points at the stale workdir, not `/home/user/sora-bridge`.

**BLOCKER**

1. **G2 metric contract mismatch: CONFIRM**
   Q1: yes, the silent-dead safety-net analysis is correct for both monitors.
   `backend/slo_monitor.py` queries `http_requests_total` and `http_request_duration_seconds_bucket`, returns `0.0` on empty Prometheus results, then `total_value <= 0` becomes healthy: [backend/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:131), [backend/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:140), [backend/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/slo_monitor.py:154).
   `backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py` has the same empty-result behavior: [backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:588), [backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:595), [backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:609).
   The app exports `/api/v1/metrics`, but via its own registry: [backend/routers/observability.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/routers/observability.py:57), [backend/metrics.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/metrics.py:962). `rg` found no exporter definitions for `http_requests_total` or `http_request_duration_seconds_bucket`; only query sites. Requirements include `prometheus-client`, not an ASGI instrumentator: [backend/requirements.in](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/requirements.in:88).
   Important correction: OP-883’s “refuse when blind” only catches Prometheus connection failures, not reachable-Prometheus-with-missing-series. Empty series still read as healthy.

2. **MISSED: systemd unit path is stale**
   The shipped unit in `/home/user/sora-bridge` would not run shipped code if installed as-is. It points to `%h/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer` for both `WorkingDirectory` and venv ExecStart: [deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service](/home/user/sora-bridge/deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service:14), [deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service](/home/user/sora-bridge/deploy/systemd/omnisight-slo-monitor.service:17).
   Given the user explicitly asked to verify shipped `/home/user/sora-bridge` instead of the stale workdir, this should be a blocker in the audit.

3. **G1 not deployed: PARTIAL/CONFIRM from filesystem, live status not confirmable here**
   `~/.config/systemd/user/` has no `omnisight-slo-monitor.service`, so the unit is not installed in the user unit directory. `systemctl --user` was blocked by this sandbox with `Failed to connect to bus: Operation not permitted`, so I cannot independently confirm `inactive`, but “not-found” is consistent.

   I found no shipped architectural reason the unit still runs OP-772. The docs actually describe OP-883 as the always-on monitor: [docs/operations/slo-runbook.md](/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/operations/slo-runbook.md:15).

5. **G3 scrape-target drift: PARTIAL**
   The audit’s “may hit nothing” is not correct for shipped compose: `backend-a` has legacy alias `backend`, preserving `backend:8000`: [docker-compose.prod.yml](/home/user/sora-bridge/docker-compose.prod.yml:195), [docker-compose.prod.yml](/home/user/sora-bridge/docker-compose.prod.yml:215).
   But it only scrapes backend-a, not backend-b. Prometheus config is still single-target: [configs/prometheus.yml](/home/user/sora-bridge/configs/prometheus.yml:7). This is replica coverage drift, not total scrape failure.

6. **G5 rollback-target coupling: PARTIAL**
   Q3: the current documented prod SOP is `scripts/deploy-prod.sh`: [docs/sop/deploy-prod-runbook.md](/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/sop/deploy-prod-runbook.md:3). It writes `OMNISIGHT_PREVIOUS_IMAGE_TAG` when the tag changes and restarts the unit if installed: [scripts/deploy-prod.sh](/home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/deploy-prod.sh:275), [scripts/deploy-prod.sh](/home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/deploy-prod.sh:281), [scripts/deploy-prod.sh](/home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/deploy-prod.sh:287).
   Release-train handoff says prod deploy is by promoted tag/digest via existing prod SOP: [docs/operations/2026-05-22-release-train-readiness-handoff.md](/home/user/sora-bridge/docs/operations/2026-05-22-release-train-readiness-handoff.md:35). So if operators follow the SOP, previous_tag is written. If they deploy manually after promotion, the audit’s no-target concern applies.

**NICE / CLEANUP**

7. **G6 canary branch dead weight: PARTIAL**
   OP-883 canary mode depends on `canary_state.json`; missing file means full rollback: [backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/orchestrator/slo_monitor.py:652). Canary rollout code and router are still shipped/wired: [backend/canary_rollout.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/canary_rollout.py:1), [backend/main.py](/home/user/sora-bridge/backend/main.py:1552).
   I did not find shipped evidence for OP-931/932/933 HOLD in the local files. Treat the “dead” claim as architecture-context dependent, not proven from code alone.

8. **G7 threshold inconsistency: CONFIRM, but intentional in comments**
   OP-772 uses 0.5% / 99.5%: [config/slos.yaml](/home/user/sora-bridge/config/slos.yaml:16).
   OP-883 uses 1%: [config/slo_thresholds.yaml](/home/user/sora-bridge/config/slo_thresholds.yaml:12).
   The OP-883 config explicitly says the split is intentional, but since only one unit is live/canonical after remediation, reconcile the source of truth.

**Fix Order**

Recommended order is mostly right, with one change:

1. Fix G2 first: create real HTTP RED metrics or rewrite monitors to shipped metrics.
2. Pick OP-883 as canonical and wire the unit to it.
3. Fix the unit path to `/home/user/sora-bridge` and shipped venv.
4. Fix Prometheus scrape coverage for both replicas.
5. Confirm prod SOP writes rollback target, then install/enable unit and add deployment-audit coverage.

Minimal-correct scope: one canonical monitor, one metric contract, one shipped-code systemd unit, both replicas scraped, previous rollback target guaranteed by the documented prod deploy path.
